[**Back to Determine verification requirements](/issuing/verification-requirements)

# Required information

To run verification checks, Adyen requires that you collect information from your account holder. The information that you need to collect is based on the account holder's legal entity type and the country/region they are operating in.

For accepted data formats, see [data formats per country/region](/issuing/verification-requirements/data-formats-per-country). You can also download requirements for all countries/regions by making a GET HTTP request [here](/flex-objects/kyc_parameters.json).

Select the legal entity type below.

![Required](/user/themes/adyen-docs/assets/icons/required.svg) Required

Minimum required information for KYC verification checks

![Conditionally required](/user/themes/adyen-docs/assets/icons/conditionally-required.svg) Conditionally required

Required information for particular setups and financial products

![Optional](/user/themes/adyen-docs/assets/icons/optional.svg) Optional

Information that is recommended to be provided

![Not applicable](/user/themes/adyen-docs/assets/icons/not-applicable.svg) Not applicable

 

Individuals  Organization  Sole proprietorship  Trust
